add_namespace = annex

#####################################
############  AFRICA   #############
#####################################


country_event = { #Fate of Morocco
	id = annex.4000
	title = annexation_morocco
	desc = annexation_morocco_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = { 
			every_state = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_morocco = yes 
					}
					add_core_of = MOR
				}
				else = {
					remove_core_of = MOR
					remove_claim_by = MOR
				}
			}
			1492 = { retain_state = yes } #Reguibat
			807 = { retain_state = yes } #Ibar Idjit
		}
	}

	#option = { #Establish West African Colonial State
	#	log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4000 option annex.establish_colony"
	#	name = annex.establish_colony
	#	trigger = {
	#		annexations_can_establish_african_colony = yes
	#	}
	#	annexations_release_BAF_colony = yes
	#}
	
	option = { #Release Morocco Whole
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4000 option annex.4000.a"
		name = annex.4000.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = MOR }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_core_of = MOR }
			remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes
		}
		release_targeted_tag = yes


		if = { 
			limit = { has_custom_puppet_MOR = yes }
			setup_custom_puppet_MOR = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = has_custom_puppet
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			MOR = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = MOR_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			MOR = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = MOR_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4000 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_morocco = yes
				is_valid_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4000 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_morocco = yes }
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4000 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		recheck_annexations = yes
    }
}

country_event = { #Fate of Reguibat
    id = annex.4001
    title = annexation_reguibat
    desc = annexation_reguibat_desc
    picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

    is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					461 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Casablanca
					920 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #El eglab
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				461 = { #Casablanca
					owner = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes }
				}
			}
			var:461.owner = { save_event_target_as = morocco }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				920 = { #El eglab
					annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
					owner = {
						NOT = { tag = event_target:morocco }
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:920.owner = { save_event_target_as = carnegia }
		}
	}

    option = { #Give it to Morocco
        log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4001 option annex.4001.a"
        name = annex.4001.a
        trigger = {
			has_event_target = morocco
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_wants_to_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:morocco }
				is_ally_with = event_target:carnegia
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				NOT = { has_event_target = carnegia }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:morocco = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:morocco }
		807 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Reguibat
    }

    option = { #Give it to Carnegia
        log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4001 option annex.4001.b"
        name = annex.4001.b
        trigger = {
			has_event_target = carnegia
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_wants_to_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:carnegia }
				is_ally_with = event_target:morocco
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				NOT = { has_event_target = morocco }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:carnegia = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:carnegia }
		807 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Reguibat
    }

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4001 option name annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		807 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Reguibat
	}

	option = { #Annex
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4001 option name annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		807 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Reguibat
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4001 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
    }

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Carnegia
	id = annex.4002
	title = annexation_carnegia
	desc = annexation_carnegia_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		807 = { retain_state = yes } #Reguibat
	}
	
	option = { #Release Carnegia Whole
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4002 option annex.4002.a"
		name = annex.4002.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = WAF }
			custom_effect_tooltip = WAF_puppet_tooltip
		}
		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			WAF = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = WAF_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			WAF = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = WAF_Republic
			}
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4002 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		recheck_annexations = yes
    }
}

country_event = { #Fate of Algerian Concessions
	id = annex.4003
	title = annexation_838
	desc = annexation_838_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = { 
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						539 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Algiers
						original_tag = FRA
						original_tag = ALG
						original_tag = BAF
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			annexations_create_baf_event_target = yes
			if = {
				limit = { 
				FRA = { 
					ROOT_can_grant_land = yes 
					NOT = { tag = event_target:baf }
				} 
			}
				FRA = { save_event_target_as = france }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					459 = { # Algiers
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = {
							NOT = { tag = event_target:france }
							NOT = { tag = event_target:baf }
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						}
					}
				}
				var:459.owner = { save_event_target_as = algiers }
			}
		}
    }

	option = { #Give to Algeirs
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4003 option annex.4003.a"
		name = annex.4003.a
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = algiers
        }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_wants_to_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:algiers }
				OR = { 
					is_ally_with = event_target:france
					is_ally_with = event_target:baf
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:algiers = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:algiers }
		838 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # 
		837 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # 
		1471 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # 
		1455 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # 
	}

	option = { #Give to British Africa
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4003 option annex.4003.b"
		name = annex.4003.b
		trigger = {
            has_event_target = baf
        }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_wants_to_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:baf }
				OR = { 
					is_ally_with = event_target:france
					is_ally_with = event_target:algiers
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:baf = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:baf }
		838 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # 
		837 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # 
		1471 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # 
		1455 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # 
	}

	option = { #Give to France
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4003 option annex.4003.b"
		name = annex.4003.c
		trigger = {
            has_event_target = france
        }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_wants_to_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:france }
				OR = { 
					is_ally_with = event_target:baf
					is_ally_with = event_target:algiers
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:france = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:france }
		838 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # 
		837 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # 
		1471 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # 
		1455 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # 
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4003 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		838 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #
		837 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } # 
		1471 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #
		1455 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4003 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		838 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } #
		837 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } # 
		1471 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } #|
		1455 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4003 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
    }
	
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { # Fate of Rif
	id = annex.4004
	title = annexation_290
	desc = annexation_290_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					173 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes }
					1483 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes }
					118 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes }
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}

		# Target 173
		if = {
			limit = {
				173 = {
					annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
					owner = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes }
				}
			}
			var:173.owner = {
				save_event_target_as = rif_target_1
				add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS }
			}
		}

		# Target 1483
		if = {
			limit = {
				1483 = {
					annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						NOT = { tag = event_target:rif_target_1 }
					}
				}
			}
			var:1483.owner = {
				save_event_target_as = rif_target_2
				add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS }
			}
		}

		# Target 118
		if = {
			limit = {
				118 = {
					annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						NOT = { tag = event_target:rif_target_1 }
						NOT = { tag = event_target:rif_target_2 }
					}
				}
			}
			var:118.owner = {
				save_event_target_as = rif_target_3
				add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Give to target 1
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4004 option target 1"
		name = annex.4004.a

		trigger = {
			has_event_target = rif_target_1
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:rif_target_1 = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:rif_target_1 }
		290 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # Rif
	}

	option = { # Give to target 2
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4004 option target 2"
		name = annex.4004.b

		trigger = {
			has_event_target = rif_target_2
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:rif_target_2 = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:rif_target_2 }
		290 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # Rif
	}

	option = { # Give to target 3
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4004 option target 3"
		name = annex.4004.c

		trigger = {
			has_event_target = rif_target_3
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:rif_target_3 = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:rif_target_3 }
		290 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # Rif
	}

	option = { # Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annexation_events.4004 option give_to_overlord"
		name = annexation_events.give_to_overlord

		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		290 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # Rif
	}

	option = { # Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4004 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration

		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}

		290 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } # Rif
	}

	option = { # Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4004 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { # Fate of Tunisia
	id = annex.4006
	title = annexation_tunisia
	desc = annexation_tunisia_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_tunisia = yes }
					add_core_of = TUN
				}
				else = {
					remove_core_of = TUN
				}
				remove_claim_by = TUN
			}

	}

		}

			}
		}
	}

		name = annex.4006.b

		trigger = {
			has_event_target = baf
		}

country_event = { #Fate of Dervish States/Somalia
	id = annex.4016
	title = annexation_559
	desc = annexation_559_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_somalia = yes }
					add_core_of = SOM
				}
				else = {
					remove_core_of = SOM
				}
				remove_claim_by = SOM
			}
			876 = { retain_state = yes } #Ogaden
			1334 = { retain_state = yes } #Jubaland
			1288 = { retain_state = yes } #Berbera
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Release Dervish States whole
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4016 option annex.4016.a"
		name = annex.4016.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = SOM }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_core_of = SOM }
			remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes
		}
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			SOM = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = SOM_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			SOM = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = SOM_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4016 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord

		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }

		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_sudan = yes }
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { # Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4016 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration

		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}

		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_sudan = yes }
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4016 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
    }

	after = { 
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4111 option annex.4111.b"
		name = annex.4111.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = ruvuma
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {

				is_ally_with = event_target:ruvuma

				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:ruvuma }
				is_ally_with = event_target:mozambique
			}

country_event = { #Fate of Zanzibar
	id = annex.4026
	title = annexation_1306
	desc = annexation_1306_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					tag = OMA
					tag = TGY
					tag = DEA
					1558 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Tanganyika
					294 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Masqat
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		
		# Tanganyika
		if = {
			limit = {
				1558 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:1558.owner = { save_event_target_as = tanganyika }
		}
		
		# Masqat
		if = {
			limit = {
				294 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						tag = OMA
					}
				}
			}
			var:294.owner = { save_event_target_as = masqat }
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Release Zanzibar
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4026 option annex.4026.a"
		name = annex.4026.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = ZAN }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			ZAN = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = ZAN_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			ZAN = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = ZAN_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to Tanganyika
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4026 option annex.4026.b"
		name = annex.4026.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = tanganyika
        }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				is_ally_with = event_target:tanganyika
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:masqat }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 10
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:tanganyika }
				is_ally_with = event_target:masqat
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:tanganyika }
        1306 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes }
	}

	option = { #Give to Masqat
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4026 option annex.4026.c"
		name = annex.4026.c
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = masqat
        }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 70
				is_ally_with = event_target:masqat
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:tanganyika }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:masqat }
				is_ally_with = event_target:tanganyika
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:masqat }
        1306 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes }
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4026 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
        1306 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes }
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4026 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
		1306 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes }
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4115 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

country_event = { #Fate of Wattaria
	id = annex.4037
	title = annexation_wattaria
	desc = annexation_wattaria_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		925 = { retain_state = yes } #Bamako
	}
	
	option = { #Release Wattaria whole
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4037 option annex.4037.a"
		name = annex.4037.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = WAT }
			custom_effect_tooltip = WAT_puppet_tooltip
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = WAT }
			release_targeted_tag = yes
		}

		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Give to British African Authority
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4037 option annex.4037.b"
		name = annex.4037.b
		trigger = {
			BAF = {
            exists = yes
			}
			ROOT = {
				tag = ENG
			}
        }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}

		BAF = {
            transfer_state = 804
        }
		BAF = {
            transfer_state = 932
        }

        recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4037 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		recheck_annexations = yes
    }
}

country_event = { #Fate of Congo / Ituri
	id = annex.4056
	title = annexation_718
	desc = annexation_718_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		295 = { retain_state = yes } #Josephstadt
		1546 = { retain_state = yes } #Kwango
		772 = { retain_state = yes } #Middle Kongo
		538 = { retain_state = yes } #Fimi
		1548 = { retain_state = yes } #Selousville
	}
	
	option = { #Release Congo whole
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4056 option annex.4056.a"
		name = annex.4056.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 80
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 10
				original_tag = GER
			}
		}
		
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = RCG }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			RCG = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = RCG_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			RCG = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = RCG_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to German Central Africa
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4056 option annex.4056.b"
		name = annex.4056.b
		trigger = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = GER
			}
        }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = DAF }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_congo_stanleyville = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4056 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_congo_stanleyville = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4056 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_congo_stanleyville = yes
			}
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4056 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	
	after = {

	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Wadi Fira
	id = annex.4057
	title = annexation_919
	desc = annexation_919_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					tag = DAR
					tag = CHA
					1527 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Ennedi
					1525 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Batha
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		
		# Ennedi
		if = {
			limit = {
				1527 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:1527.owner = { save_event_target_as = ennedi }
		}
		
		# Batha
		if = {
			limit = {
				1525 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:1525.owner = { save_event_target_as = batha }
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Give to Ennedi
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4057 option annex.4057.a"
		name = annex.4057.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = ennedi
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 65
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:ennedi }
				OR = { 
					is_ally_with = event_target:batha
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:ennedi = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:ennedi }
        919 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes }

		}

			base = 65

				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}

				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:batha }

					is_ally_with = event_target:ennedi
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:batha = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}

country_event = { #Fate of Tananariva/Madagascar
	id = annex.4083
	title = annexation_543
	desc = annexation_543_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		708 = { retain_state = yes } #Comoros Islands
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					tag = FRA
					tag = ENG
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Release Madagascar
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4083 option annex.4083.a"
		name = annex.4083.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 90
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = MAD }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			MAD = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = MAD_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			MAD = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = MAD_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4083 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
        every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_madagascar = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4083 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_madagascar = yes
			}
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4083 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Darfur
	id = annex.4084
	title = annexation_767
	desc = annexation_767_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Africa

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		# Dongola owner
		if = {
			limit = {
				1281 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:1281.owner = { save_event_target_as = dongola }
		}
		
		# Kordofan owner
		if = {
			limit = {
				549 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:549.owner = { save_event_target_as = kordofan }
		}
		
		#
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					1281 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Dongola
					549 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Kordofan
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Release Darfur
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4084 option annex.4084.a"
		name = annex.4084.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				BEF = {
					exists = yes
				}
				ROOT = {
					has_subject = BEF
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = DAR }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			DAR = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = DAR_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			DAR = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = DAR_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Grant it to Kordofan
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4084 option annex.4084.b"
		name = annex.4084.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = kordofan
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				BEF = {
					exists = yes
				}
				ROOT = {
					has_subject = BEF
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:kordofan }
        767 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #North Darfur
		1527 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Ennedi
		1528 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Wadi
	}

	option = { #Grant it to Dongola
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4084 option annex.4084.c"
		name = annex.4084.c
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = dongola
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				BEF = {
					exists = yes
				}
				ROOT = {
					has_subject = BEF
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:dongola }
        767 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #North Darfur
		1527 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Ennedi
		1528 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Wadi
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord

country_event = { #Fate of Kaapstadt/South Africa
	id = annex.4085
	title = annexation_856
	desc = annexation_856_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Africa

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					original_tag = HOL
					original_tag = SWA
					original_tag = BOT
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		
		# Bloemfontain owner
		if = {
			limit = {
				858 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
					OR = {
						is_owned_by = SWA
						is_owned_by = BOT
					}
				}
			}
			var:858.owner = { save_event_target_as = bloemfontain }
		}
		
		# Bechuanaland owner
		if = {
			limit = {
				1318 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:1318.owner = { save_event_target_as = bechuanaland }
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Release an autonomous South Africa
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4085 option annex.4085.a"
		name = annex.4085.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			NOT = {
				ROOT = {
					original_tag = HOL
					original_tag = SDW
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 30
				original_tag = GER
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = PRE }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			PRE = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = PRE_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			PRE = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = PRE_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Install a colonial administration in South Africa
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4085 option annex.4085.b"
		name = annex.4085.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			ROOT = {
				OR = {
					is_on_continent = europe
					original_tag = RUS
					original_tag = SDW
					original_tag = DAF
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 50
				original_tag = ENG
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 60
				original_tag = GER
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				original_tag = SDW
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				original_tag = DAF
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				original_tag = HOL
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = PRE }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					original_tag = ENG
				}
			}
			PRE = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = PRE_ENG_Colonial
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					original_tag = GER
					original_tag = SDW
					original_tag = DAF
				}
			}
			PRE = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = PRE_GER_Colonial
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					original_tag = HOL
				}
			}
			PRE = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Transfer it to Bloemfontain
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4085 option annex.4085.c"
		name = annex.4085.c
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = bloemfontain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				factor = 40
				ROOT = {
					original_tag = ENG
					is_ally_with = SWA
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:bloemfontain }
        every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_south_africa = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Transfer it to Bechuanaland

			is_subject = no

country_event = { #Fate of Stallaland
	id = annex.4089
	title = annexation_1571
	desc = annexation_1571_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Africa

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					275 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Transvaal
					858 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Bloemfontain
					1318 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Bechuanaland
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		
		# Transvaal owner
		if = {
			limit = {
				275 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:275.owner = { save_event_target_as = transvaal }
		}
		
		# Bloemfontain owner
		if = {
			limit = {
				858 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:858.owner = { save_event_target_as = bloemfontain }
		}
		
		# Bechuanaland owner
		if = {
			limit = {
				1318 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:1318.owner = { save_event_target_as = bechuanaland }
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Transfer it to Transvaal
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4089 option annex.4089.a"
		name = annex.4089.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = transvaal
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 90
				AND = {
					NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:bloemfontain }
					NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:bechuanaland }
				}
				is_ally_with = event_target:transvaal
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:transvaal }
				OR = {
					is_ally_with = event_target:bloemfontain
					is_ally_with = event_target:bechuanaland
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:transvaal }
        1571 = {
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Transfer it to Bloemfontain
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4089 option annex.4089.b"
		name = annex.4089.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = bloemfontain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 90
				AND = {
					NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:transvaal }
					NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:bechuanaland }
				}
				is_ally_with = event_target:bloemfontain
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:bloemfontain }
				OR = {
					is_ally_with = event_target:transvaal
					is_ally_with = event_target:bechuanaland
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:bloemfontain }
        1571 = {
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Transfer it to Bechuanaland
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4089 option annex.4089.c"
		name = annex.4089.c
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = bechuanaland
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 70
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				AND = {
					NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:transvaal }
					NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:bloemfontain }
				}
				is_ally_with = event_target:bechuanaland
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 10
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:bechuanaland }
				OR = {
					is_ally_with = event_target:transvaal
					is_ally_with = event_target:bloemfontain
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:bechuanaland }
        1571 = {
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4089 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
        1571 = {
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4089 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
		1571 = {
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4089 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Swaziland
	id = annex.4090
	title = annexation_859
	desc = annexation_859_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Africa

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		1316 = { retain_state = yes } #Zululand
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					original_tag = PRE
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		
		# Winburg owner
		if = {
			limit = {
				1570 = {
					is_owned_by = PRE
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:1570.owner = { save_event_target_as = winburg }
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Release Swaziland
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4090 option annex.4090.a"
		name = annex.4090.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			NOT = {
				ROOT = {
					original_tag = PRE
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 90
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = SWA }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			SWA = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = SWA_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			SWA = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = SWA_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Transfer it to Winburg
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4090 option annex.4090.b"
		name = annex.4090.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = winburg
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				factor = 60
				PRE = {
					exists = yes
				}
				ROOT = {
					OR = {
						is_ally_with = PRE
						has_subject = PRE
					}
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:winburg }
        every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_swaziland = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4090 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
        every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_swaziland = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4090 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_swaziland = yes
			}
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4090 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Khomas/Namibia
	id = annex.4095
	title = annexation_1567
	desc = annexation_1567_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Africa

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		1319 = { retain_state = yes } #Kalahari
		542 = { retain_state = yes } #Tswanaland
		1566 = { retain_state = yes } #Okavango
		1561 = { retain_state = yes } #Kwando
		540 = { retain_state = yes } #Namibe
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					original_tag = SDW
					original_tag = PRE
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Release Namibia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4095 option annex.4095.a"
		name = annex.4095.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			NOT = {
				ROOT = {
					original_tag = SDW
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 90
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = NMB }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			NMB = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = NMB_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			NMB = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = NMB_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4095 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
        every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_namibia = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4095 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_namibia = yes
			}
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4095 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

			has_event_target = kasanje

		ai_chance = {
			base = 50

			}

country_event = { #Fate of Zimbabwe
	id = annex.4101
	title = annexation_545
	desc = annexation_545_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Africa

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					original_tag = MZB
					original_tag = ZIM
					1326 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Sofala
					1324 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Matabeleland
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		# Sofala owner
		if = {
			limit = {
				1326 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:1326.owner = { save_event_target_as = sofala }
		}
		
		# Matabeleland owner
		if = {
			limit = {
				1324 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:1324.owner = { save_event_target_as = matabeleland }
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Release Capeloland
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4101 option annex.4101.a"
		name = annex.4101.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			NOT = {
				ROOT = {
					original_tag = MZB
					original_tag = ZIM
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = CAP }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			CAP = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = CAP_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			CAP = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = CAP_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Transfer it to Sofala
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4101 option annex.4101.b"
		name = annex.4101.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = sofala
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 90
				AND = {
					NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:matabeleland }
				}
				is_ally_with = event_target:sofala
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 10
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:sofala }
				OR = {
					is_ally_with = event_target:matabeleland
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:sofala }
        545 = {
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Transfer it to Matabeleland
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4101 option annex.4101.b"
		name = annex.4101.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = sofala
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 70
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				AND = {
					NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:sofala }
				}
				is_ally_with = event_target:matabeleland
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:matabeleland }
				OR = {
					is_ally_with = event_target:sofala
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:matabeleland }
        545 = {
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4101 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
        545 = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
				}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4101 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
		545 = {
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel

		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

country_event = { #Fate of Barotseland
	id = annex.4104
	title = annexation_868
	desc = annexation_868_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Africa

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {

			limit = {

					is_country_pink_map = yes

				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		# Kasanje owner
		if = {
			limit = {
				1321 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						tag = PEF
					}
				}
			}
			var:1321.owner = { save_event_target_as = kasanje }
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Release Barotseland
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4104 option annex.4104.a"
		name = annex.4104.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			NOT = {
				ROOT = {
					original_tag = PEF
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 90
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = BAR }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			BAR = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = BAR_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}

				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = BAR_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}

		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = kasanje

			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 90
				is_ally_with = event_target:kasanje
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 10
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:kasanje }
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:kasanje }
        868 = {
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4104 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes

				limit = {
					is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
				}
				transfer_state_and_notify = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}

		}
	}

		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4108 option annex.4108.b"

			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = kasai
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 70
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 90
				AND = {
					NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:chokweland }
				}
				is_ally_with = event_target:kasai
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:kasai }
				OR = {
					is_ally_with = event_target:chokweland
				}
			}
		}

        every_owned_state = {

				is_garanganza = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes

	}

country_event = { #Fate of Kasanje
	id = annex.4109
	title = annexation_1321
	desc = annexation_1321_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Africa

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_country_pink_map = yes
					874 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Luanda
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		
		# Luanda
		if = {
			limit = {
				874 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						tag = PEF
					}
				}
			}
			var:874.owner = { save_event_target_as = luanda }
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Release Angola
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4109 option annex.4109.a"
		name = annex.4109.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			NOT = {
				ROOT = {
					original_tag = PEF
				}
			}
			ANG = {
				exists = no
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 90
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = ANG }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			ANG = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = ANG_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			ANG = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = ANG_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Transfer it to Luanda
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4109 option annex.4109.b"
		name = annex.4109.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = luanda
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 90
				is_ally_with = event_target:luanda
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 10
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:luanda }
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:luanda }
        every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_angola = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4109 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
        every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_angola = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4109 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				annexations_can_annex = yes

				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		
		# Amhara
		if = {
			limit = {
				1284 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:1284.owner = { save_event_target_as = amhara }
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Release Afar
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4116 option annex.4116.a"
		name = annex.4116.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			NOT = {
				ROOT = {
					original_tag = ETH
					original_tag = DJI
					original_tag = RET
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = ERI }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			ERI = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = ERI_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			ERI = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = ERI_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Transfer it to Amhara
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4116 option annex.4116.b"
		name = annex.4116.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = amhara

			base = 70

				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 90
				is_ally_with = event_target:amhara
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 10
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:amhara }
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:amhara }
        550 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes }
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4116 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
